[@dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller](../index.md) / Roll

# Class: Roll\<T\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:76](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L76)

One evaluated notation: the result tree and its totals.

Can be used on its own, or through a `Roller`, which adds rolls made by `roll()` to its history.

`total` is calculated straight away; `minTotal` / `maxTotal` evaluate the expression again in
`min` / `max` mode on first access, and are cached (or taken from the data on import). Each
evaluation gets a fresh generated-roll budget per pool; when it is spent, repeating modifiers
return the dice rolled so far instead of throwing for the limit. The readonly properties do not
make the result tree or shared settings deeply immutable.

## Example

```ts
const roll = new Roll("4d6kh3");   // rolls straight away
roll.total;                        // e.g. 14
`${roll}`;                         // e.g. "4d6kh3: [3d, 5, 6, 3] = 14"

new Roll("4d6", { engine: { name: "mersenne", seed: 42 } });
```

## Type Parameters

### T

`T` = `string`

the output type of the configured presenter

## Constructors

### Constructor

> **new Roll**\<`T`\>(`input`, `options?`): `Roll`\<`T`\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:99](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L99)

Parses (if a string) and rolls the input. `RollData` is restored as it is, without rolling or
validating: its result and totals are used, and `reroll()` rolls its expression. Use
`Roll.import()` / `Roll.fromJSON()` for exported data, which validate it.

#### Parameters

##### input

`string` \| [`ExpressionNode`](../type-aliases/ExpressionNode.md) \| [`RollData`](../interfaces/RollData.md)

the notation or expression to roll, or data to restore without validation

##### options?

[`RollSettings`](RollSettings.md)\<`T`\> \| [`RollOptions`](../interfaces/RollOptions.md)\<`T`\>

how to roll; `RollSettings` are used as they are, so they can be shared

#### Returns

`Roll`\<`T`\>

#### Throws

the notation is invalid

### total

> `readonly` **total**: `number`

## Accessors

### averageTotal

#### Get Signature

> **get** **averageTotal**(): `number`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:189](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L189)

The expression evaluated with each pool's quantity times its die's average.
Uses the stored die snapshots without rolling again or rounding intermediate averages.

Rolls containing modifiers retain the midpoint of `minTotal` and `maxTotal` for now.
This is not a statistical mean for modifiers, non-linear maths or random die parameters.
Its uncached extrema use fresh per-pool budgets and include any partial results when exhausted.

##### Returns

`number`

***

### maxTotal

#### Get Signature

> **get** **maxTotal**(): `number`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:177](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L177)

Total with each die at its maximum, rounded to 2 decimal places; not necessarily an expression maximum (e.g. `-d6`). Modifier-generated rolls stop at the fresh per-pool budget.

##### Returns

`number`

***

### minTotal

#### Get Signature

> **get** **minTotal**(): `number`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:172](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L172)

Total with each die at its minimum, rounded to 2 decimal places; not necessarily an expression minimum (e.g. `-d6`). Modifier-generated rolls stop at the fresh per-pool budget.

##### Returns

`number`

## Methods

### export()

#### Call Signature

> **export**(`format`): `object`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:223](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L223)

Exports as JSON (default), a JSON-safe object, or UTF-8 base64 JSON. Uncached min/max totals use fresh per-pool budgets and truncate generated rolls at the limit.

###### total

> `readonly` **total**: [`EncodedNumber`](../type-aliases/EncodedNumber.md)

###### type

> `readonly` **type**: `"roll"`

###### version

> `readonly` **version**: `1`

#### Call Signature

> **export**(`format?`): `string`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:224](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L224)

Exports as JSON (default), a JSON-safe object, or UTF-8 base64 JSON. Uncached min/max totals use fresh per-pool budgets and truncate generated rolls at the limit.

##### Parameters

###### format?

`"json"` \| `"base64"`

##### Returns

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:225](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L225)

Exports as JSON (default), a JSON-safe object, or UTF-8 base64 JSON. Uncached min/max totals use fresh per-pool budgets and truncate generated rolls at the limit.

`"object"` \| `"json"` \| `"base64"`

### reroll()

> **reroll**(`options?`): `Roll`\<`T`\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:195](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L195)

Rolls the original input again; an engine override applies only to this reroll. Does not add to a history.

### toJSON()

> **toJSON**(): `object`

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:218](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L218)

JSON-safe export data with non-finite numbers tagged; uncached min/max totals use fresh per-pool budgets and truncate generated rolls at the limit.

### toString()

> **toString**(): `string`

### fromJSON()

> `static` **fromJSON**\<`T`\>(`data`, `options?`): `Roll`\<`T`\>

Defined in: [src/Roll.ts:134](https://github.com/dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller/blob/next/src/Roll.ts#L134)

Creates a roll from exported data (e.g. from `toJSON()`), without rolling.

The options are used by `reroll()`. Pools refer to their die by factory name, so re-rolling
uses the die registered under that name in these settings.

#### Type Parameters

##### T

`T` = `string`
